                <div n="42" type="adhyāya">
                    <head xml:lang="en">[Adhyāya 42, draft based on MS H]</head>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.1"><l>
                        
                        
                         athāto rasaviśeṣavi-jñānīyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.42.1]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.2"><l/></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.3"><l>
                        
                        
                         ākāśapavanadahanatoyabhūmiṣu yathāsaṅkhyam ekottaraparivṛddhāḥ ||
                        śa bdasparśarūparasagandhāḥ || tasmād āpyo rasaḥ
                        parasparānupraveśāc ca sarvveṣāṃ sānnidhyam asti utkarṣāt tu
                        grahaṇaṃ | sa khalvāpyo rasaḥ śeṣabhūtasaṃsarggādvidagdhaḥ
                        ṣaḍvidho bhavati | tad yathā | madhuro 'mlo lavaṇaḥ kaṭukastiktaḥ
                        kaṣāya iti | tatrodakavāhulyātmadhuraḥ | toyāgnivāhulyādamlaḥ
                        bhūmyagnivāhulyāl lavaṇaḥ vāyvākāśavāhulyāttiktaḥ |
                        vāyvagnivāhulyātkaṭukaḥ pṛthivyanilavāhulyātkaṣāya iti || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.42.3]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.4"><l>
                        
                        
                         tatra madhurāmlalavaṇā vātaghnāḥ | madhuratiktakaṣāyāḥ pittaghnāḥ
                        kaṭutiktakaṣāyāḥ śleṣmaghnāḥ || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.42.4]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.5"><l>
                        
                        
                         tatra vāyurātmaivātmā | pittamāgneyaṃ | śleṣma saumya iti || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.42.5]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.6"><l>
                        
                        
                         ta ete rasāḥ svayonivardhanā anyayonipraśamanāś ca | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.42.6]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.7"><l>
                        
                        
                         kecid āhur agnīṣomīyatvājjagataḥ | dvividhā rasāḥ saumyā āgneyāś
                        ca | tatra madhuratiktakaṣāyāḥ saumyāḥ dvividhā kaṭvamlalavaṇā
                        āgneyāḥ | madhurāmlalavaṇāḥ snigdhā guravaś ca dvividhā
                        kaṭutiktakaṣāyā rūkṣā laghavaś ca | saumyāḥ śītāḥ | āgneyās tūṣṇāḥ
                        | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.42.7]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.8.1"><l>
                        
                        
                         tatra śaityarūkṣalāghavavaiṣadyaguṇalakṣaṇo vāyuḥ | tasya
                        samānayoniḥ kaṣāyo rasaḥ | saumyaḥ śaityāc chaity am
                        abhivardhayati | raukṣyād raukṣyaṃ lāghavāl lāghavaṃ vaiṣadyād
                        vaiṣadyam iti || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1.42.8.1]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.8.2"><l>
                        
                        
                         auṣṇyataikṣṇyaraukṣyalāghavavaiṣadyaguṇalakṣaṇampittantasya
                        samānayoniḥ kaṭuko rasaḥ | saumya auṣṇyād auṣṇyam abhivardhayati |
                        taikṣṇyāt taikṣṇyaṃ raukṣyād raukṣyaṃ lāghavāl lāghavaṃ vaiśadyād
                        vaiśadyam iti || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1.42.8.2]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.8.3"><l>
                        
                        
                         mādhuryasnehagauravaśaityapaicchilyaguṇalakṣaṇaḥ śleṣmā tasya
                        samānayo nirmadhuro rasaḥ | saumya mādhuryāt mādhuryam
                        abhivardhayati | snehāt snehaṃ gauravād gauravaṃ śaityāc chaityaṃ
                        paicchilyāt paicchilyam iti ||</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1.42.8.3]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.8.4"><l>
                        
                        
                         tasya punar vviparītaḥ kaṭuko rasaḥ | śleṣmaṇaḥ pratyanīkatvāt |
                        kaṭukyātmādhuryam abhibhavati | raukṣyāt snehaṃ lāghavād gauravaṃ
                        auṣṇyāt paicchity an tad etan nidarśanamātram uktaṃ | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.42.8.4]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.9"><l>
                        
                        
                         rasalakṣaṇam ata ūrdhvaṃ vakṣyāmaḥ || tatra yaḥ paritoṣam
                        utpādayati tarppayati mukhopalepañ janayati prahlādayati śleṣmāṇañ
                        cābhivardhayati sa madhuraḥ || yo dantaharṣam utpādayati |
                        mukhāśrāvañ jana-yati śraddhāñ cotpādayati so 'mlaḥ || yo
                        bhaktarucim utpādayati | kaphaprasekañ janayati mārddavañ
                        copādayati sa lavaṇaḥ || yo jihvām udvejayati śiro gṛhṇāti nāsikāñ
                        ca śrāvayati sa kaṭukaḥ || yo gale śoṣam utpāda-yati
                        mukhavaiṣadyam utpādayati bhaktaruciñ copādyati sa tiktaḥ || ya
                        āsyam pariśoṣayati- jihvāṃ stambhayati kaṇṭham badhnāti hṛdayam
                        pīḍayati sa kaṣāyaḥ || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.42.9]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.10.1"><l>
                        
                        
                         rasaguṇān ata ūrdhvam vakṣyāmaḥ || tatra madhuro raso
                        raktamāṃsado 'sthimajjojaḥ śukravardhanaś cākṣuṣyaḥ
                        keśyauvalakṛtsandhānaḥ śoṇitaḥ śoṇitaprasādo
                        vālavṛddhakṣatakṣīṇahitaḥ ṣaṭpadapipīlikānāmiṣṭatamaḥ ||
                        tṛṣṇāmūrcchādāhaḥ praśamanaḥ | ṣaḍindriyaprasādanaś ceti | sa
                        evaṃguṇo 'py eka evātyartham upayujyamānaḥ kāsaḥ śvāso
                        lasakavamathurvvadanamādhuryasvaropaghātakṛmigalagaṇḍān āpādayati
                        | tathārvvudaślīpadabastigudopalepābhiṣyandaprabhṛtīn
                        nayanavikārānn upajayati || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1.42.10.1]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.10.2"><l>
                        
                        
                         amlas tu jaraṇaḥ pācanapavananigrahaṇo nulomano vidāhī vahiḥśītaḥ
                        kledanaḥ prāyaśo hṛdyaś ceti | sa khalv evaṃguṇo 'py eka
                        evātyartham upayujyamāno
                        dantaharṣanayanasammīlanaromasamvejanakaphavilāyanaśarīrapraśithilatām
                        āpādayati | tathā
                        kṣatavihatadagdhadaṣṭabhagnaśū-nacyutāvamathitacchiyavisarppitacchinnaviddhotpiṣṭādīni
                        pāca-yaty āgneyasvabhāvatvvāt || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1.42.10.2]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.10.3"><l>
                        
                        
                         lavaṇas tu saṃśodhanaḥ pācano viśleṣaṇas tarppaṇaḥ kledanaḥ
                        śaithilyakṛt sarvvarasapratyanīkabhūto mārggaviśodhanaḥ
                        sarvvaśarīrāvayavamārddavakaraś ceti | sa khalu evaṃguṇo 'py eka
                        evātyartham upasevyamāno gātrakaṇḍūko tha śophavaivarṇṇakaraḥ
                        svaropaghātendriyopatāpān upajanayati |
                        tathākṣimukhapākaraktapittavātaśoṇitāmlīkāprabhṛtīnvikārān
                        upajanayati || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1.42.10.3]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.10.4"><l>
                        
                        
                         kaṭukas tu dīpanaḥ pācano rocanaḥ śodhanaḥ sthaulyā
                        lyālasakaviṣakuṣṭhakaṇḍūpraśamanaḥ sandhibandhacchedano vasādanas
                        stanyaśukrakaphamedasām upahantā ceti | sa khalv evaṃguṇo 'py eka
                        evātyartham upayujyamāno bhramamadagalatālvoṣṭhaśoṣadāhasantāpān
                        āpādayati | tathā valavighātakampatodabhedakṛt
                        karacaraṇapārśvapṛṣṭhaprabhṛtiṣu ca vātaśūlān upajanayati || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1.42.10.4]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.10.5"><l>
                        
                        
                         tiktas tu rocano dīpanaḥ śodhanaḥ kaṇḍūkoṣṭhatṛṣṇāmūrcchāpraśanaḥ
                        stanyaśodhano viṇmūtrakledamedovasāpūyopaśoṣaṇaś ceti | sa khalv
                        evaṅguṇo 'py eka evātyartham upayujyamāno
                        gātramanyāstambhākṣepakordditaśiraḥśūlān upa-janayati | tathā
                        bhramatodabhedacchedāsyavairasyāny āpādayati || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1.42.10.5]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.10.6"><l>
                        
                        
                         kaṣāyas tu saṅgrāhiko ropaṇaḥ stambhanaḥ śodhanollekhanaḥ śoṣaṇaḥ
                        pīḍanaḥ kledopaśoṣaṇaś ceti | sa khalv evaṅguṇo 'py eka
                        evātyartham upayujyamāno
                        hṛdayapīḍāsyaśoṣodarādhmānavākyagrahamanyāstambhaprabhṛtīn vikārān
                        upajanayati | tathā
                        gātrasphuraṇacimicimāyanākuñcanākṣepaṇaprabhṛtīn
                        vikārānupajanayati || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1.42.10.6]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.11"><l>
                        
                        
                         sarvveṣām eva dravyāṇy upadekṣyāmaḥ | tad yathā kākolyādi
                        kṣīraghṛtavasāmajjaśāliyavagodhūmamāṣaśṛṅgāṭakakaśeruka
                        kālāṅkālukapiyālapuṣkaravījakāśmaryamadhūkadrākṣākharjūrarājādananālikerekṣuvikārāḥ
                        |
                        balātibalātmaguptāvidārīpayasyāgokṣurakakṣīramoraṭamadhūlikākūṣmāṇḍādiḥ
                        samāsena madhuro varggaḥ ||
                        dāḍimāmalakamātuluṅgāmrāmrātakakapitthakaramarddavadaraprācīnāmalakakośāmrabhavyapārāvatavetraphalatintiḍīkalajacāmlavetasadantaśaṭhadadhitakrasurāsauvīratuṣodakadhānyāmlaprabhṛtīni
                        samāsenāmlo varggaḥ ||
                        saindhavasauvarccalaviḍapākyaromakasāmudrapācimakṣāroṣarasuvarccilaprabhṛtīni
                        samāsena lavaṇo varggaḥ || pippa-lyādiḥ surasādir
                        mmadhuśigrumūlakalaśunasumukhaśītaśivakuṣṭhadevadāruhareṇukā
                        'valgujaphalacaṇḍāguggulumustalāṅgalakīśukanāsīpīluprabhṛtī-ni
                        śālasārādiś ca prāyaśaḥ kaṭuko varggaḥ ||
                        āragvadhādirgguḍūcyādirmmaṇḍūkaparṇṇīvetrakarīraharidrādvayendrayavavaruṇasvādukaṇṭakasaptaparṇṇavṛhatīdvayaśaṃkhinītṛvṛtkṛtavedhanakarkkoṭakakāravellavārttākukaravīrasumanāḥśaṃkhapuṣpyapāmārggatrāyamāṇāśokarohivaijayantīsuvarccalāvṛścikālījyotiṣmatīprabhṛtīni
                        tikto varggaḥ || nyagrodhādirambaṣṭhādir lodhrādiḥ
                        priyaṅvādistriphalā śallakījamvvasthitindukādīni katakaśākaphalāni
                        sālasārādiś ca prāyasaḥ
                        kuravakakovidārajīvantīsuniṣaṇṇakaprabhṛtīni varakādayo mudgādayaś
                        ca samāsena kaṣā-yo varggaḥ || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.42.11]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.12"><l>
                        
                        
                         tatraiteṣāṃ rasānāṃ saṃyogās triṣaṣṭirv bhavanti || tad yathā
                        pañcadaśa dvikāḥ | viṃśatis trikāḥ | pañcadaśa catuṣkāḥ | ṣaṭ
                        pañcakāḥ | ekaṣaṭkāḥ | ekaikaśaś ca śaḍrasā iti | teṣām anyatra
                        vakṣyāmaḥ || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.42.12]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.13"><l>
                        
                        
                         bhavati cātra ||
                        <caesura/>
                         yuktāḥ ṣaḍadhigacchanti valino vaśyatāṃ rasāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         yathā doṣāḥ prakupitā vaśaṃ yānti valīyasa iti ||</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.42.13]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.trailer"><l> || 42 ||</l></lg>
                </div>
                <div n="43" type="adhyāya">
                    <head xml:lang="en">[Adhyāya 43, draft based on MS H]</head>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.43.1"><l>
                        
                        
                         athāto vamanadravyavikalpavijñānīyam vyākhyāsyāmaḥ ||</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.43.1]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.43.2"><l/></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.43.3.part1"><l>
                        
                        
                         vamanadravyānām phalākhyānām madhye madanaphalāni śreṣṭhatamāni
                        bhavanti || tatra madanapuṣpāṇām ātapaśuṣkāṇā cūrṇaprakuñcaṃ
                        pratyak puṣpīnimvakaṣāyāṇām anyatamenālo pāyayitvā vāmayet |
                        madanaśatvāṭūcūrṇṇāny evam madhulavaṇayuktāny abhiprataptāni |
                        madanaśalāṭucūrṇṇasiddhām vā tilayavāgūn | nivṛttām vā nātihariti
                        pāṇḍūnāṃ kuśamūḍhāvavaddhamṛdgomayapraliptānāṃ
                        yavavūsamāṣaśālyādidhānyarāśāvaṣṭharātroṣitaklinnabhinnānām
                        phalapippalīrāhṛtyātape pariśoṣayet tāsāṃ
                        dadhipalavimṛditapariśuṣkāṇāṃ subhājanasthānām antarnnakhamuṣṭhim
                        uṣṇena yaṣṭhīmadhukaṣāyeṇa kovidārādhyanyatamakāṣāyeṇa vā vimṛdya
                        trirātraparyuṣitam madhusaindhavayuktam āśīrbhir abhimantritam
                        udaṅmukhaḥ prāṅmukham āturam pāyayet anena mantreṇa abhimantrya
                        ||</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1.43.3.part1]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.43.3.1"><l>
                        
                        
                         brahmadakṣāśvirūdrendrabhūcandrārkkānilānalāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         ṛṣayaḥ sauṣadhigrāmā bhūtasaṃghāś ca pāntu te ||<anchor xml:id="SS.1.43.3.1-n1"/>
                    </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1.43.3.1]</trailer>
</lg>
                    

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.43.3.2"><l>
                        
                        
                         rasāyaneva siddhānān devānām amṛtaṃ yathā |
                        <caesura/>
                         sudhevottamanāgānāsbhaiṣajyam idam astu te ||</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1.43.3.2]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.43.3.part2"><l>
                        
                        
                         viśeṣeṇa śleṣmajvarapratiśyāyāntarvvidradhiṣv apravarttamāne doṣe
                        pippalīvacāsarṣapakalkonmiśrair uṣṇāmvubhiḥ punaḥ punaḥ
                        pravarttayed āsamyag vāntalakṣaṇād iti | madanaphalamajjacūrṇṇam
                        vā tat kvāthaparibhāvitam vasena dravyakaṣāyeṇa | madanaphalamajjā
                        siddham vā payaḥ | madanaphalamajjasiddhena vā payasā vā yavāgūm
                        adhobhāgāsṛhṛdrogayoḥ | madanaphalamajjasiddhasya vā payaso
                        dadhibhāvam upagatasya | dadhyuttarakaṃ dadhi vā
                        kaphaprasekaśarddistameṣu | madanaphalamajjācūrṇṇa rasam vā
                        bhallātakasenhavadādāya phāṇitībhūtaṃ lehayet | ātapaśuṣkam vā
                        jīvantī kaṣāyeṇa pittakaphasthānagate | madanaphala majjākvātham
                        vā pippalyādi prativāpaṃ ūṣaṇanimvakaṣāya yor anyatamena
                        santarppaṇaṃ | kaphasarvvavyādhiharam iti madanaphalam uktaṃ | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1.43.3.part2]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.43.4"><l>
                        
                        
                         jīmūtakakusumacūrṇṇam vā pūrvvavad eva kṣīreṇa nivṛtteṣu yavāgūṃ
                        romaseṣu | santānikām vā nirllomaśeṣu | dadhyuttarakaṃ
                        haritakapāṇḍuṣu | dadhi tat kaṣāyasaṃsṛṭāṃ surām vā paryāgateṣu |
                        madanaphalamajja </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.43.4]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.43.5"><l>
                        
                        
                         vidhānavat kuṭajaphalamajjavidhānaṃ | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.43.5]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.43.6"><l/></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.43.7"><l>
                        
                        
                         ikṣvākukusumacūrṇṇam vā pūrvvavad eva kṣīreṇa | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.43.7]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.43.8"><l>
                        
                        
                         dhāmārgavasyāpi madanaphalamajjā vidhānavad upayogaḥ || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.43.8]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.43.9"><l>
                        
                        
                         kṛtavedhanamadanaphalamajjapippalīmām vamanadravyaparibhāvitānām
                        bahuśaś cūrṇṇam utpalādiṣu dattam āghrā ya vasati
                        tattvanavavaddhadoṣeṣu yavāgūmākaṇṭham pītavatsu ca dadyād iti |
                        śirovirecanāny evaṃ pradhānatamāni bhavanti || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.43.9]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.43.10"><l/></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.43.11"><l>

                        <caesura/>
                         kalkaiḥ kaṣāyaiḥ svarasaiś ca cūrṇṇair api ca vuddhimān |
                        <caesura/>
                         peyalehyādibhojyeṣu vamanānyūpakalpayet || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.43.11]</trailer>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.1"><l>
                        
                        
                         athāto virecanadravyavikalpavijñānīyam vyākhyāsyāmaḥ ||</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.44.1]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.2"><l/></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.3"><l>
                        
                        
                         aruṇābhan tṛvṛn mūlaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ mūlavirecane |
                        <caesura/>
                         pradhānas tilvakas tv akṣu phaleṣv api harītakī ||</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.44.3]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.4"><l>
                        
                        
                        <caesura/>
                         snehāpayaḥ payassūktam iti prādhānyasaṅgrahaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         teṣām vidhānaṃ vakṣyāmi yathāvad anupūrvvaśaḥ ||</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.44.4]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.5"><l>
                        
                        
                         vairecanadravyakaṣāyapītam mūlam
                        <caesura/>
                         mahattraivṛtamatra śuddhaṃ |
                        <caesura/>
                         cūrṇṇīkṛtaṃ saindhavanāgarāḍhyam
                        <caesura/>
                         pibet tad amlair anilādhijuṣṭaḥ ||</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.44.5]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.6"><l>
                        
                        
                         svādukvāthair api cekṣor vvikāraiḥ paitteroge kṣīrayuktān
                        nihanyāt |
                        <caesura/>
                         drākṣārasatriphalākvāthamūtrair yuktam pibet kaphajavyoṣagāḍhaṃ
                        ||</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.44.6]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.7"><l>
                        
                        
                         trivarṇṇakatrikaṭukābhyānyuktaṃ lihyāc cūrṇṇan tadguḍenābhiyojyaṃ
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         kvāthaprasthe kuḍavan tasya dadyāt yuktyā dadyān nāgaraṃ
                        saindhavañ ca ||</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.44.7]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.8"><l>
                        
                        
                         pacet sarvvaṃ yāvad etad ghanaṃ syāl lehībhūtan tatprayojyaṃ
                        tatas tu |
                        <caesura/>
                         tṛvṛtkalkonāgarārddhena yuktaḥ sasaindhavo mūtrayuktas tu peyaḥ
                        ||</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.44.8]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.9"><l>
                        
                        
                         sametṛvṛn nāgare cābhayāñ ca dadyād arddham pūgaphalaṃ sadāruḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         viḍaṃgasāraṃ marica vaiṣayogaḥ sasaindhavo mūtrayuktaḥ pradhānaḥ
                        ||</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.44.9]</trailer>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.10"><l/></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.12"><l>
                        
                        
                         vairecanadravyacūrṇṇasya bhāgas tataḥ kvāthaḥ sammitañcāsya
                        tulyaṃ |
                        <caesura/>
                         sumardditaṃ sarppiṣā tacchritena tatkvāthośmasveditā varttitañ ca
                        ||</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.44.12]</trailer>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.13"><l>

                        pramādāt |
                        <caesura/>

                        |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.44.13]</trailer>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.14"><l>

                        saindhavāḍhyaḥ ||
                        <caesura/>
                         virecayed vaidaleṣv eṣu kalpaḥ kāryās tajñair vvāmanīyeṣu caiva
                        ||</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.44.14]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.15"><l>
                        
                        
                         ikṣur dvidhā pāṭayitvāvalipya tṛvṛt kalkaiḥ pravisandhāya cāpi |
                        <caesura/>
                         paktvā samyak puṭapākaṃ krameṇa khādec chītaṃ pittarogābhibhūtaḥ
                        ||</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.44.15]</trailer>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.32"><l>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.35"><l>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.36"><l>

<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.44.36]</trailer>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.37"><l>

<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.44.37]</trailer>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.38"><l>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.39"><l>

<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.44.39]</trailer>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.40"><l>

                        <caesura/>

<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.44.40]</trailer>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.41"><l>
                        
                        
                         bhāgaṃ saṃkṣudya saṃsṛjya yavaiḥ sthālyāṃ sahākṣipet |
                        <caesura/>

<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.44.41]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.42"><l>
                        
                        
                         susiddhāṃ cāvacāryaitām oṣadhibhyo vimokṣayet |
                        <caesura/>

<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.44.42]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.43"><l>
                        
                        
                         pūrvvoktauṣadhabhāgasya cūrṇṇaṃ datvā |
                        <caesura/>
                         tenaiva sahayūṣeṇa kalase pūrvva sukṣipet ||</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.44.43]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.44"><l>
                        
                        
                         jñātvā jātarasañ cāpi tattuṣodakam āpnuyāt |
                        <caesura/>
                         tuṣodasauvīrakayor vvidhir eṣa prakīrttitaḥ ||</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.44.44]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.45"><l>
                        
                        
                         ṣaḍrāttrāt saptarātrād vā te ca peye sure smṛte |
                        <caesura/>
                         vairecaneṣu dravyeṣu tṛvṛtkalpavidhiḥ smṛtaḥ ||</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.44.45]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.82"><l>
                        
                        
                         mahāvṛkṣapayaḥ pītair yavāgūstaṇḍulaiḥ kṛtāḥ |
                        <caesura/>

<trailer xml:lang="en">[1.44.82]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.83"><l>

</lg>

                        <caesura/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.66"><l>

                        <caesura/>
                         līḍhvā guḍaiṇa salilam paś cād uṣṇam piven naraḥ |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.44.66]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.67"><l>
                        
                        
                         pippalyādi kaṣāyeṇa pibet piṣṭāṃ harītakīṃ ||
                        <caesura/>
                         saindhavopahitāṃ samyag eṣa yogo virecanaḥ |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.44.67]</trailer>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.44.56cd"><l>

                <div n="45" type="adhyāya">
                    <head xml:lang="en">[Adhyāya 45, draft based on MS H]</head>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.1"><l>
                        
                        
                         athāto dravadravyaviśeṣavijñānīyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.45.1]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.2"><l/></lg>
                    <!-- <lg type="varga"> -->
                    <p ana="subheading" style="bold">[Pānīyavarga]</p>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.3"><l>
                        
                        
                         pānīyaṃm āntarīkṣyam anirddeśyarasam amṛtaṃ jīvanan tarppaṇan
                        dhāraṇam āśvāsajananaṃ śramaklamapipāsāmadamūrcchātandrāpraśamanam
                        ekāntataḥ pathyatamañ ca || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.45.3]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.4"><l>
                        
                        
                         tad evāvanau patitam anyatamam upalabhyate |
                        nadīsarastaḍāgakūpavāpīpraśravaṇodbhijacauṇṭyādiṣu sthāneṣv
                        anyatamaṃ rasam upabhate | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.45.4]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.5"><l>
                        
                        
                         tatra lohitakapilapāṇḍupītanīlaśukleṣv avanipradeśeṣu
                        madhurāmlalavaṇakaṭutiktakaṣāyā yathāsaṃkhyam udakarasā bhavantīty
                        eke bhāṣante || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.45.5]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.6"><l>
                        
                        
                         tat tu na samyak pṛthivyādīnām anyo 'nyānupraveśakṛtaḥ sa
                        khalūdakaraso bhavaty utkarṣāpakarṣakṛtena saṃyogaviśeṣeṇa ||
                        tatra svalakṣaṇodakaguṇabhūyiṣṭhāyām madhuraṃ |
                        toyāgniguṇabhūyiṣṭhāyām amlaṃ| pṛthivyagniguṇabhūyiṣṭhāyāṃ lavaṇaṃ
                        | vāyvagniguṇabhūyiṣṭhāyāṅ kaṭukaṃ| vāyvākāśaguṇabhūyiṣṭhāyāṃ
                        tiktaṃ | pṛthivyanilaguṇabhūyiṣṭhāyāṅ kaṣāyaṃ |
                        ākāśaguṇabhūyiṣṭhāyām avyaktarasam avyaktaṃ hy ākāśam ity atas tat
                        pradhānam avyaktarasatvāt tat peyam āntarīkṣālābhe | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.45.6]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.7"><l>
                        
                        
                         tatrānta-rikṣañ caturvvidhaṃ || tadyāthā | dhāraṃ kāraṃ tauṣāraṃ
                        haimam iti | teṣān dhāraṃ pradhānaṃ laghutvāt tat punar dvividhaṃ
                        | gāṅgaṃ sāmudrañ ca tayoḥ parīkṣaṇaṃ | tatra śālyodanapiṇḍam
                        akuthitam avidagdhaṃ | rajatabhājanopahitaṃ varṣati deve kurvvīta
                        sa cet muhūrttasthitas tādṛśa eva bhavati | tad gāṅgam iti
                        avagantavyaṃ | varṇṇānyatāsitthaprakledo vā tatsāmudram iti vidyāt
                        | tan nopādeyaṃ | sāmudram apy āśvayuji māsi gṛhītaṃ gāṅgavad
                        bhavati | tad upādeyam iti | śuklapaṭaikadeśapracyutam athavā
                        harmmyatalaparibhraṣṭam anyair vvā prayogair ggṛhītaṃ śailabhājane
                        śailavan mṛnmaye vā pātreṣv anuguptan nidadhyāt | tat sarvvakālam
                        upayuñjīta | tasyālābhe bhaumaṃ | tat punaḥ saptavidhaṃ | tad
                        yathā kaupan nādeyaṃ sārasan tāḍāga m prāsravaṇam audbhijaṃ
                        cauṇṭyam iti || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.45.7]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.8"><l>
                        
                        
                         tatra varṣāvarṣāsvāntarikṣamaudbhijaṃ vā seveta mahāguṇatvā-t |
                        śaradi sarvvam prasannatvāt | hemante sārasan tāḍāgam vā | vasante
                        kaupaṃ cauṇṭyaṃ prāsrāvaṇam vā | grīṣme 'pyevaṃ | prāvṛṣyendram
                        anabhivṛṣṭaṃ sarvvaṃ eva | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.45.8]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.9"><l>
                        
                        
                         kīṭamūtrapurīṣāṇḍaśava-kothapradūṣitaṃ | tṛṇaparṇṇodakayutaṃ
                        kaluṣam viṣasaṃyutaṃ || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.45.9]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.10"><l>
                        
                        
                         yo 'vagāheta varṣāsu pibed vāpi navañ jalaṃ |
                        <caesura/>
                         sa bāhyābhyantarān rogān labhate kṣipram eva tu || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.45.10]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.11"><l>
                        
                        
                         tatra yat paṅkaśevālatṛṇahaṭhapadmaprabhṛtibhir avacchannaṃ
                        raviśaśikiraṇānilair vvātijuṣṭāṃ gandharasopasṛṣṭan tad vyāpannam
                        iti vidyāt | sparśarasarūpagandhavīryavipākāḥ | doṣāḥ ṣaṭ kharatā
                        paicchilyam auṣṇyaṃ dantagrāhitā ca sparśadoṣaḥ |
                        paṅkasikatāśaivālavāhulyādvikṛtavarṇṇatā rūpadoṣaḥ | vyaktarasatā
                        rasadoṣaḥ | aniṣṭagandhatā gandhadoṣaḥ | yad upayuktan
                        tṛṣṇāgauravaśūlakaphaprasekān āpādayati sa vīryadoṣaḥ | yad
                        upayuktaṃ cirād vipacyate viṣṭambhayati sa vipākadoṣaḥ || ta ete
                        āntarikṣe doṣā na bhavanti | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.45.11]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.12"><l>
                        
                        
                         vyāpanne cāgnikvathanaṃ sūryātapanam ayam piṇḍataptanirvvāpaṇam
                        vā prasādhakam bhavati</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.45.12]</trailer>
</lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.13"><l/></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.14"><l/></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.15"><l/></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.16"><l/></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.17"><l>
                        
                        
                         saptakaluṣasya prasādhakāni bhvanti | tad yathā ||
                        katakagomedakavisagranthiparṇṇīmūlaśevālavastrāṇimuktāmaṇiś ceti
                        || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.45.17]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.19"><l>
                        
                        
                         saptaśītīkaraṇāni bhavanti | tad yathā || pravātasthāpanam
                        dakaprakṣepanaṃ yaṣṭibhrāmaṇam vyajanaṃ | vastroddharaṇaṃ |
                        vālukāprakṣepaṇaṃ sikyāvalam vanañ ceti || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.45.19]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.18"><l>
                        
                        
                         pañca nikṣepaṇāni bhavanti | tad yathā | phalakaṃ tryaṣṭakaṃ
                        muñjāvalayaṃ dakamañ cikāśikyakañ ceti | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.45.18]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.12a"><l>
                        
                        
                         nāgapuṣpacampakotpalapāṭalāprabhṛtibhiś cādhivāsanam iti || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.45.12a]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.20"><l>
                        
                        
                         sugandhavispaṣṭarasaṃ suśītan tarṣanāśanaṃ |
                        <caesura/>
                         acchaṃ laghu ca hṛdyañ ca toyaṅ guṇavad ucyate || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.45.20]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.21"><l>
                        
                        
                         tatra nadyaḥ paścimābhimukhāḥ pathyā laghūdakatvāt |
                        pūrvvābhimukhā na praśasyante gurūdakatvāt | dakṣiṇābhimukhā
                        nātidoṣalā bhavanti | sādhāraṇatvāt | tatra sahyaprabhavāḥ kuṣṭhañ
                        janayanti | vindhyaprabhavā kuṣṭhaṃ hṛdrogañ ca | malayaprabhavāḥ
                        krimīn | mahendraprabhavāḥ ślīpadodarāṇi | himavatprabhavāḥ |
                        galagaṇḍaṃ kvacid gaṇḍamālāṃ | prācyāvanty āparāvantyāś cārśāṃsy
                        upajanayanti | tatra pāriyātraprabhavā valārogyakarā iti || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.45.21]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.22"><l>
                        
                        
                         nadyaḥ śīghravahā laghvyaḥ proktā yāś cāmalodakāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         gurvvyaḥ śaivālakaluṣajalaudyāmandagāś ca yāḥ || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.45.22]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.23"><l/></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.24"><l>
                        
                        
                         tatra sarvveṣāṃ bhaumānām pratyūṣasi grahaṇaṃ | tatra hi
                        śaityalāghavam adhikam bhavati | śaityañ cāparo guṇa iti || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.45.24]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.25"><l>
                        
                        
                         divārkkakiraṇair jjuṣṭaṃ juṣtam indukarair nniśi |
                        <caesura/>
                         arūkṣam anabhiṣyandi tattulyaṃ gaganāmbunā || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.45.25]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.26"><l>
                        
                        
                         gaganāmbu tridoṣaghnaṃ gṛhītaṃ yat subhājane |
                        <caesura/>
                         balyaṃ rasāyanaṃ śītaṃ mātrāpekṣan tataḥ paraṃ || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.45.26]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.27ab"><l>
                        
                        
                         raktoghnaṃ śītalaṃ hlādi jvaradāhastṛṣāpahaṃ | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.45.27ab]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.28"><l>
                        
                        
                         mūrcchāpittoṣmadāheṣu viṣe rakte mādatyaye ||
                        <caesura/>
                         bhramaklamaparīteṣu tamake vamathau tathā | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.45.28]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.29"><l>
                        
                        
                         ūrddhage raktapitte ca śītamambhaḥ praśasyate ||
                        <caesura/>
                         pārśvaśūlapratiśyāye vātaroge galagrahe | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.45.29]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.30"><l>
                        
                        
                         ādhmāte stimite kāṣṭhe sadyaḥśuddhe navajvare ||
                        <caesura/>
                         hikkāyāṃ snehapīte ca śītāmbu parivarjjayet | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.45.30]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.45ab"><l/></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.45cd"><l>
                        
                        
                         arocake pratiśyāye pramehaś ca yathau tathā || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.45.45cd]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.46"><l>
                        
                        
                         mande 'gnāvudare koṣṭhe jvare netrāmayeṣu ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         vraṇe ca madhumehe ca pānīyam mandam ācaret || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.45.46]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.46.1"><l/></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.47.1"><l/></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.27cd"><l>
                        
                        
                         candrakāntabhavam vāri pittaghnam vimalaṃ smṛtam | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.45.27cd]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.33ab"><l/></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.33cd"><l>
                        
                        
                         sakṣāramprāyasaḥ kaupan nādeyaṃ kīrttitan tathā|| </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.45.33cd]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.32ab"><l/></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.32cd"><l>
                        
                        
                         tāḍākam vātalaṃ rūkṣaṃ sārasañ caiva tādṛśaṃ | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.45.32cd]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.32.1"><l>
                        
                        
                         autsam asmasam āśleṣāduṣṇaṃ pittena śasyate || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1.45.32.1]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.35.1"><l>
                        
                        
                         sarvvadā sarvvadoṣeṣu pathyam prāsrāvaṇam payaḥ | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1.45.35.1]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.45.35ab"><l>
                        
                        
                         avidāhy udbhijaṃ toyam pittaghnam madhuraṃ smṛtaṃ || </l>

                <div n="46" type="adhyāya">
                    <head xml:lang="en">[Adhyāya 46, draft based on MS H]</head>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.1"><l>
                        
                        
                         athāto 'nnapānavidhim vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.1]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.2"><l/></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.3"><l>
                        
                        
                         dhanvantarim abhivādya suśruta uvāca ||
                        <caesura/>
                         bhagavan prāgabhihitam prāṇināṃ mūlam āhāro balavarṇṇaujasāñ ca
                        saḥ ṣaṭsu raseṣv āyatto rasāḥ punar dravyāśrayiṇaḥ |
                        dravyarasavīryavipākanimitte ca kṣayavṛddhī doṣāṇāṃ sātmyañ ca |
                        brahmāder api ca lokasyāhāraḥ sthityutpattihetur
                        āhārādevābhivṛddhir balam ārogyavarṇendriyaprasādaś ca | tathā hy
                        āhāravaiṣampādasvāsthyan tasyāsitapītalīḍhakhāditasya
                        nānādravyātmakasyānekavidhavikalpaprabhāvasya pṛthak pṛthak
                        dravyarasavīryavipākakarmmecchāṃ jñātuṃ na hy
                        anavabuddhadravyasvabhāvā bhiṣajo roganigrahaṅ karttuṃ samarthā
                        ity āhārāyattāś ca prāṇino yasmāt tasmād annapānavidhim upadiśat
                        tu me bhagavān ity uktaḥ provāca bhagavān dhanvantariḥ
                        <caesura/>
                         || atha khalu vatsa suśruta || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.3]</trailer>
</lg>
                    <!-- <lg type="varga"> -->
                    <p ana="subheading" style="bold">[<hi>Dhānyavarga</hi>]</p>
                    <p ana="subsubheading" style="bold">[<hi>Śālivarga</hi>]</p>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.4"><l>
                        
                        
                         tatra
                        lohitakaśālikalamakakarddamakapāṇḍukasugandhakasakunāhṛtakuṣmāṇḍakapuṇḍarīkamahāśāliśītabhīrukalodhraśūkadīrghaśūkakāñcanakahāyanakadūṣīmahākhyaprabhṛtayaḥ
                        śālayaḥ || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.4]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.5"><l>
                        
                        
                         madhurā vīryataḥ śītā vipākā kaṭukāḥ smṛtāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         pittaghnālpānilakaphaḥ snigdhā vaddhālpavarccasaḥ || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.5]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.6"><l>
                        
                        
                         teṣāṃ lohitakaḥ śreṣṭho doṣaghnaḥ śukramūtralaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         cakṣuṣyo varṇṇavalakṛt svaryo hṛdyas tṛṣāpahaḥ || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.6]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.7ab"><l/></lg>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.7cd"><l>
                        
                        
                         tasmād alpāntaraguṇāḥ kramaśaḥ śālayo pare || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.7cd]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.8"><l>
                        
                        
                         ṣaṣṭikakāṅgakamukundakapītakapramodakakālakāsanapuṣpakamahāsvetakamahāvrīhicūrṇṇakakuravakedāraprabhṛtayo
                        vrīhayaḥ || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.8]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.9"><l>
                        
                        
                         rase pāke ca madhurāḥ pittānilaharāḥ smṛtāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         śālīnāṅguṇataś cāpi samānā vaddhavarccasaḥ || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.9]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.10"><l>
                        
                        
                         ṣaṣṭikaḥ pravaras teṣāṅ kaṣāyānuraso laghuḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         mṛduḥ snigdhas tridoṣaghnaḥ sthairyakṛdvalavarddhanaḥ || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.10]</trailer>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.53"><l>
                        
                        
                         ata ūrddhvam māṃsavarggam upadekṣyāmaḥ ||
                        <caesura/>
                         tatra jaleśayā anūpā grāmyāḥ | kravyabhuja | ekasaphajāṅgalāś
                        ceti | ṣaṇmāṃsavarggā bhavanti | teṣām uttarottarāḥ pradhānatamās
                        te punar dvividhā ānūpajāṅgalāś ca | tatra jāṅgalavarggo
                        'ṣṭavidhaḥ | tatra jaṃghālā viṣkirāḥ pranudā guheśayāḥ prasahāḥ
                        parṇṇamṛgāḥ | vileśayā grāmyāś ceti || teṣañ jaṅghālaviṣkirau
                        pradhānatamau | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.53]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.54"><l>

                        kaṣāya madhurā laghavo vātapittaharās tīkṣṇā bastiviśodhanāḥ || </l>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.69"><l>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.139"><l>
                        
                        
                         ata ūrdhvaṃ phalāny upadekṣyāmaḥ ||
                        <caesura/>
                         tad yathā || dāḍimāmalakavadarakolakarkandhukapitthamātuluṅgām
                        pramāṇaṃ mrāmrātakalakucakara
                        marddanabhavyapiyālapārāvatavetraphalaprācīno
                        malakatintiḍīkanīpakuśāmrāmlītakanāgaraṅgaprabhṛtīni |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.139]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.139.add"><l>

</lg>

                         phalamāṃseṣuvikṛtīstilamāṣopasaṃskṛtāḥ || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.407]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.408"><l>
                        
                        
                         bhakṣyā valyātha guravo vṛṃhaṇā hṛdayapriyāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         kapālāṅgārapakvāḥ syuḥ kiñcillaghutarās tu te || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.408]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.409"><l>
                        
                        
                         sakilāṭādayo bhakṣyā guravaḥ kaphavardhanāḥ |
                        <caesura/>

<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.409]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.410"><l>
                        
                        
                         udāvarttaharo vādyaḥ pratisyāmehanāśanaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         dhānālumbās tu laghavaḥ kaphamedoviśeṣaṇāḥ || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.410]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.411"><l>
                        
                        
                         śaktavastarppaṇā hṛdyās tṛṣṇāpittakaphāpahāḥ |
                        <caesura/>

<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.411]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.412"><l>

                        <caesura/>

<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.412]</trailer>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.413"><l>

                        <caesura/>

<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.413]</trailer>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.414"><l/></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.415"><l>

                        <caesura/>

<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.415]</trailer>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.416"><l>

                        <caesura/>

<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.416]</trailer>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.417"><l>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.417.add1"><l>

                        <caesura/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.419"><l>

                        <caesura/>
                         amlena kecid vihatā manuṣyā mādhuryayoge praṇayībhavanti |
                        <caesura/>

                        || </l>

                        || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.420]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.421"><l>

                        | </l>

</lg>



                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.422"><l>

<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.425]</trailer>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.432"><l>
                        
                        
                         ataḥ paraṃ tu vargāṇām anupānam pṛthak pṛthak |
                        <caesura/>
                         pravakṣyāmy anupūrvveṇa sarvveṣām eva me śṛṇu || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.432]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.433"><l>
                        
                        
                         tatra pūrvvaśasya jā tānām vedarāmlaṃ | vaidalānān dhāmlaṃ |
                        jāṅgalānān mṛgānān dhanvajānām pakṣiṇāñ ca pippalyāsavaḥ |
                        viṣkirāṇāṃ kolavadarāsavaḥ | pratudānāṃ kṣīravṛksāsavaḥ | guhe
                        śayānāṃ kharjjūranālikerāsavaḥ | sāmudrāṇām mātuluṅgāsavaḥ |
                        kuṣmāṇḍānām amlānām sārdvīkāsavaḥ | amlā amlānāṃ phalānāṃ
                        padmotpalakandāsavaḥ | kaṣāyāṇāṃ dāḍimavetrāsavaḥ | madhurāṇāṃ
                        trikaṭukayuktaḥ khadirāsavaḥ | tālaphalādīnāṃ dhānyāmlaṃ |
                        kandānāṃ dūrvvānalavetrāsavaḥ | pippalyādīnāṃ
                        svadaṃṣṭrāvasukāsavaḥ | cuccūprabhṛtīnāṃ lodhrāsavaḥ |
                        kusumbhaśākasya tad eva | maṇḍūkaparṇyādīnām mahāpañcamūlyāsavaḥ |
                        tālamastakādīnām amlaphalāsavaḥ | saindhavādīnāṃ surāsavam
                        āranālan toyam vā || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.433]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.434"><l>
                        
                        
                         bhavanti cātra ślokāḥ ||
                        <caesura/>
                         sarvveṣām anupānānāṃ māhendran toyam uttamaṃ |
                        <caesura/>
                         sātmyam vā yasya yat toyaṃ tatsmai hitam ucyate || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.434]</trailer>
</lg>



                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.435"><l>
                        
                        
                         doṣavad guru vā bhuktam atimātram athāpi vā || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.435]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.436"><l>

<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.436]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.437"><l>

<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.437]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.438"><l>

<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.438]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.439"><l>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.440"><l>

</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.441"><l>

                        <caesura/>

<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.441]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.442"><l>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.444"><l>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.446"><l>

                        <caesura/>
                         āptāsthitam asaṃkīrṇṇe śuci kāryaṃ mahānasaṃ | </l>

</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.447"><l>

                        <caesura/>
                         śucau deśeṣu saṃguptaṃ samupasthāpayed bhiṣak | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.447]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.448"><l>

                        <caesura/>
                         siddhair mantrair hataviṣaṃ siddham annan nidedayet || 0 || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.448]</trailer>

                    <!-- <lg type="varga"> -->
                    <p ana="subheading" style="bold">[<hi>Āhāropakalpana: How to serve
                            food</hi>]</p>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.449"><l>
                        
                        
                         vakṣyāmyataḥ paraṃ kṛtsnām āhārasyopakalpanāṃ |
                        <caesura/>
                         ghṛtaṃ kārṣṇāyase deyaṃ peyā deyā tu rājate || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.449]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.450"><l>
                        
                        
                         phalāni sarvvabhakṣyāṃś ca pradayā vaidaleṣu tu |
                        <caesura/>
                         pariśuṣkaṃ pradigdhāni sauvarṇṇeṣūpahārayet || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.450]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.451"><l>
                        
                        
                         dravāṇi rasāś caiva rājateṣūpahārayet |
                        <caesura/>
                         kaṭvarāṇi khalāś caiva sarvvāñ cchaileṣu dāpayet || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.451]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.452"><l>
                        
                        
                         dadyāt tāmramaye pātresu śītaṃ suśritaṃ payaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         pānīyam pānakam madyam mṛnmayeṣu pradāpayet || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.452]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.453"><l>
                        
                        
                         kācasphaṭikapātreṣu śītaleṣu śubheṣu |
                        <caesura/>
                         vajravaidūryacitreṣu rāgaṣāḍavaṣadūkān || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.453]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.454"><l>
                        
                        
                         purastād vimale pāre suvistīrṇṇe manorame |
                        <caesura/>
                         sūdaḥ sūpodanan dadyāt pradehāś ca su saṃskṛtaṃ || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.454]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.455"><l>
                        
                        
                         phalāni sarvva bhaksāś ca pariśuṣkāṇi yāni ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         tāni dakṣiṇapārśve tu bhuñjānasyopakalpayet || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.455]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.456"><l>
                        
                        
                         pradravāṇi rasāś caiva pānīyam pānakam payaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         khalān yūṣāś ca peyāś ca sarvve pārśve pradāpayet || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.456]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.457"><l>
                        
                        
                         sarvvān guḍavikārāś ca rāgaṣāḍavaṣaṭūkān |
                        <caesura/>
                         purasthāt sthāpayet prājño dvayor api ca madhayataḥ || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.457]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.458"><l>
                        
                        
                         evaṃ vijñāya matimān bhojanasyopakalpanāṃ |
                        <caesura/>
                         bhoktāram vijane ramye niḥsampāte śubhe śucau || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.458]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.459"><l>
                        
                        
                         sugandhapuṣparacite same deśe 'tha bhojayet |
                        <caesura/>
                         viśiṣṭam iṣṭasaṃskāraiḥ pathair hṛdyair asādibhiḥ || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.459]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.460"><l>
                        
                        
                         manojñaṃ śuci nātyuṣṇaṃ pratyagram aśanaṃ hitaṃ |
                        <caesura/>
                         pūrvvam madhuram aśnīyāl lavaṇomlautaḥ paraṃ || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.460]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.461"><l>
                        
                        
                         paścād eṣān rasān vai dyo bhojaneṣ eva cārayet |
                        <caesura/>
                         pādau phalāni yuñjīta dāḍimādīni vuddhimān ||</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.461]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.462"><l>
                        
                        
                         tataḥ peyān tato bhojyāñś citrāṃs tataḥ paraṃ |
                        <caesura/>
                         ghanaṃ pūrvvaṃ samaśnīyād iti kecid avasthitāḥ || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.462]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.463"><l>
                        
                        
                         ādāvante ca madhye ca bhojane ca praśasyate |
                        <caesura/>
                         niratyayan doṣaharam phaleṣv āmalakaṃ nṛṇāṃ || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.463]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.464"><l>
                        
                        
                         mṛṇālavisaśālūkakandekṣuprabhṛtīni tu |
                        <caesura/>
                         pūrvvaṃ yojyāni bhiṣajā na tu bhukte kathañcana || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.464]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.465"><l>
                        
                        
                         sukham uccaiḥ samāsīnaṃ samadehonnatatparam |
                        <caesura/>

                    <p ana="subheading" style="bold">[<hi>Metabolic process</hi>]</p>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.526"><l>
                        
                        
                         ata ūrddham pravakṣyāmi āhāragatiniścayaṃ |
                        <caesura/>
                         pañcabhūtātmake dehe āhāraḥ pākabhautikaḥ ||
                        <caesura/>
                         vipakvaḥ pañcadhā samyag guṇāṃs tān abhivarddhayet ||</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.526]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.527"><l>
                        
                        
                         avidagdhaṃ kaphaṃ pittam vidagdhaḥ pavanam punaḥ ||
                        <caesura/>
                         samyag vipakṣo niḥsāra āhāraḥ paribṛṃhayet |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.527]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.528"><l>
                        
                        
                         viṇmūtram āhāramalaḥ sāraḥ prāgīrito rasaḥ ||
                        <caesura/>
                         sa tu vyānena vikṣiptaḥ sarvvān dhātūn visarppati |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.528]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.529"><l>
                        
                        
                         kaphapittamalaḥ kheṣu svedaḥ syān nakharoma ca ||
                        <caesura/>
                         netraviṭ tvakṣu ca sneho dhātūnāṃ kramaśo malāḥ |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.529]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.530"><l>
                        
                        
                         divāvivuddhahṛdaye jāgrataḥ puṇḍarīkavat ||
                        <caesura/>
                         anupaklinna dhātvannam ajīrṇṇe 'pi hitaṃ niśi |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.530]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.531"><l>
                        
                        
                         hṛdayaṃ līyate rātrau prasuptasya viśeṣataḥ ||
                        <caesura/>
                         samupaklinnadhātvannam ajīrṇṇe tvahitaṃ divā ||</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.531]</trailer>
</lg>
                    <!-- </lg> -->

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.532"><l>
                        
                        
                         imam vidhiṃ yo 'numatam mahāmuner
                        <caesura/>
                         mmaharṣimukhyasya paṭhet tu yatnataḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         sa bhūmipālo ya vidhātum auṣadham
                        <caesura/>
                         mahātmanāñ cārhati vaidyasattama iti || o ||</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 1.46.532]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.532.add1"><l>
                        
                        
                         dravyajñānaṃ rasajñānam vasanañ ca virecanaṃ |
                        <caesura/>
                         dravadravyaparijñānam annapānena ṣaṭ smṛtaḥ || o ||</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1.46.532.add1]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.532.add2"><l>
                        
                        
                         sūtrasthāne purā proktañ catvāriṃśat ṣaḍuttaraṃ |
                        <caesura/>
                         adhyāyāḥ kāśirājena pūrṇṇaṃ sarvvaṃ savistaraṃ ||</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1.46.532.add2]</trailer>
</lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.trailer"><l>|| iti suśrute śalyatantre sūtrasthānaṃ
                        samāptaṃ || ||</l></lg>

<standOff corresp="#SS.1.39.12" type="Notes">
<note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.1.39.12-n1">Ḍalhaṇa noted the
                                alternative reading <foreign>avyāpaddurbalasyāpi</foreign>
                                that appears in our witnesses.</note>
</standOff>
<standOff corresp="#SS.1.41.5" type="Notes">
<note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.1.41.5-n1">The word
                                    <foreign>kārmuka</foreign> is known in early medical
                                texts from this instance and one in the
                                    <title>Carakasaṃhitā</title> 1.26.13. The word is
                                formed by P.5.1.103 in the sense “capable of accomplishing
                                an action."</note>
</standOff>
<standOff corresp="#SS.1.43.3.1" type="Testimonia">
<note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.1.43.3.1-n1">This and the
                                next passage are found in the <title>Agnipurāṇa</title>
                                279.014cd (Mitra 1870: v.3, p.35).</note>
</standOff>
<standOff corresp="#SS.1.45.65.add1" type="Notes">
<note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.1.45.65.add1-n1">The <foreign xml:lang="sa">dadhivarga</foreign> (vulgate verses
                                66-83) is not present in the Nepalese version.</note>
</standOff>
<standOff corresp="#SS.1.45.84" type="Notes">
<note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.1.45.84-n1">The <foreign xml:lang="sa">takravarga</foreign> (vulgate verses
                                84-95) is mostly not present in the Nepalese
                                version.</note>
</standOff>
<standOff corresp="#SS.1.45.96" type="Notes">
<note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.1.45.96-n1">The <foreign xml:lang="sa">ghṛtavarga</foreign> (vulgate verses
                                97--109) is mostly not present in the Nepalese
                                version.</note>
</standOff>
<standOff corresp="#SS.1.45.112" type="Notes">
<note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.1.45.112-n1">The <foreign xml:lang="sa">tailavarga</foreign> (vulgate verses
                                112--131) is shorter in the Nepalese version.</note>
</standOff>
<standOff corresp="#SS.1.45.132" type="Notes">
<note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.1.45.132-n1">The <foreign xml:lang="sa">madhuvarga</foreign> (vulgate verses
                                132-147) is mostly not present in the Nepalese
                                version.</note>
</standOff>
<standOff corresp="#SS.1.45.148" type="Notes">
<note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.1.45.148-n1">The <foreign xml:lang="sa">ikṣuvarga</foreign> (vulgate verses
                                148-169) is much abbreviated in the Nepalese
                                version.</note>
</standOff>
<standOff corresp="#SS.1.45.170ab" type="Notes">
<note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.1.45.170ab-n1">The <foreign xml:lang="sa">madyavarga</foreign> (vulgate verses
                                170-216) is much abbreviated in the Nepalese
                                version.</note>
</standOff>
<standOff corresp="#SS.1.46.296abcd" type="Notes">
<note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.1.46.296abcd-n1">The
                                    <foreign>kandavarga</foreign> that follows in the
                                vulgate (1931 ed., passages 298-312) is not present in the
                                Nepalese version.</note>
</standOff>
<standOff corresp="#SS.1.46.504" type="Notes">
<note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.1.46.504-n1">The Nepalese
                                version is in <foreign>upajāti</foreign> metre, with the
                                fifth line in <foreign>śloka</foreign>. The vulgate omits
                                the middle lines and makes a simple
                                    <foreign>śloka</foreign> of the whole.</note>
